# Changelog

## [2026-10-17 17:59] (wall clock) M9c: Tier B call recall, scip-go image, cross-module scip-go joins
- **scip-go image (PI, B13b's cause):** rb's image built scip-go 0.2.7 with Go 1.27.2, which writes export data version 5, against x/tools v0.45.0, which reads only up to version 4. Every import failed to load, silently (exit 0), so every scip-go index rb had made came from a degraded type check: 119,977 of server's 202,385 locals were `invalid type`. The image is now scip-go v0.2.7 on x/tools v0.50.0, tagged `rb-scip-go:0.2.7-xtools0.50.0`, with `go.mod`/`go.sum` pinned by sha256 and a build-time self-test that fails unless scip-go types `os.Getenv` (its control fails on the old image). The 322 scip-go `IMPLEMENTS` on mattermost that the degraded type check related (277 from `*Suite#` types, the judged-FALSE `ElasticsearchInterfaceTestSuite#` row among them) left with the image, so D7 is withdrawn (C8; `relationships_unverified` never built).
- **Fixes** (contract `tasks/contract-m9c.md`):
  - **B3 (TS):** tsconfig `paths` are read as JSONC (comments, trailing commas), and default imports resolve to the target's `export default`.
  - **B2 (Go):** receivers typed through call results (`T` and `(T, error)`), call segments in a qualifier, type aliases, embedded structs (breadth-first, never `IMPLEMENTS`) and sibling-file package vars.
  - **B5 (Python):** `super()`, star imports (with the `_` and `__all__` rules), re-exports, module variables, `raise A` and inherited `__init__`.
  - **B1 (D8′):** a scip-go reference into another of the repository's Go modules joins that job's definition by symbol, version dropped, as a `synthetic` `scip` edge; `scip_cross_project` counts `synthetic`, `ambiguous` and `unanchored`, and `exact calls (scip)` counts `exact` only.
  - Every reference resolved through a new form is counted in a per-file `tier_b_forms` diagnostic (11 keys). All new Tier B edges are `heuristic`. `PIPELINE` `m9c-1`.
- **P7b (perf fix):** the SCIP join parsed a symbol at every occurrence, and the `scip` crate's parser formats its state with `Debug` per descriptor. A per-join memo parses each distinct symbol once; output unchanged. SCIP-mode CPU against M9b went 1.297× → 0.975×.
- **Scoreboard** (`bench/scoreboard/m9c.json`, binary sha256 `f340b267…06c9` from `55592a1`, `PIPELINE` `m9c-1`; run `var/bench/out/f340b267d529/`):
  - scip `calls` agreement P 78.4 % → 99.5 %, R 62.4 % → 96.3 %. mattermost gold 33,312 → 43,840, tp 20,640 → 42,181, fp 6,141 → 203, fn 12,672 → 1,659.
  - bookcars `calls` tp 1,155 → 1,841, fn 694 → 8; go_clean_arch 49 of 49; go_gin_example 161 of 161.
  - PyCG `calls` R 25.0 % → 29.2 % (14 of 48), `calls_file_scope` R 50.5 % → 56.5 % (105 of 186).
  - 17 of 314 cells changed. `compare` against the re-scored M9b record exits 0 with 6 accepted lines over 5 cells, all pairs with an end in files SCIP does not cover, plus mattermost file-scope fn 6 → 8 (new gold).
  - `exact` 100.0 % of 126, unchanged; the `scip` sample redrew 54 edges, judged blind: 54 TRUE, 60 of 60.
  - Invariant over 157 dumps: B1 23,187, IMG 455, B2 42,784, B3 8,896, B5 118, RTA 20, DIAG 8,787 rows, 0 unclassified; no Tier B edge `exact`.
  - Of the 203 mattermost fp, 185 are version skew (server compiles against `server/public v0.4.3`), M9d's.
- **Perf:** mattermost `noscip-fresh` A/B against M9b: CPU 1.010×, wall 1.059×, RSS 1.056×, noise. SCIP mode 0.975× CPU after P7b. Five small-target wall warnings re-measured A/B: noise.
- **Verification:** real-Docker 1,153/0/0 at P6; rb-indexer 375 and rb-scip 82 at P7b; fmt and clippy clean; mutants m9c 38/38, m9b 39/39, m9a 31/31, m8b 29/29 KILLED; two scorings `cmp`-identical; `rb.repo` unchanged.
- **Files:**
  - **Code:** `crates/rb-scip/src/{jobs,lib,symbol}.rs`, `crates/rb-indexer/src/{lib,resolve,rta,scip}.rs`, `crates/rb-syntax/src/{lib,bindings,decorations,queries,keys}.rs`, `crates/rb-store/src/{query,db_tests}.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/src/doctor.rs`, `docker/scip-go/{Dockerfile,README.md}`, goldens `crates/rb-cli/tests/golden/{go-chi-sqlx,polyglot-monorepo}/scip-status.txt`.
  - **Bench:** `bench/probe/m9c_forms.py`, `bench/mutants/m9c.json`, `bench/samples/exact.tsv`, `bench/scoreboard/m9c{,-perf}.json`, `bench/accept-m9c.tsv`.
  - **Docs:** `README.md`, `docs/{usage-and-testing,repository-brain-design}.md`.
  - **Records:** `tasks/{contract-m9c,contract-m9d,bench-findings,todo,lessons,plan-m9}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-16] (wall clock) Mutant battery: parallel runs and a result cache
- `bench/mutants/run.py` takes `--jobs N`: each spec runs in its own detached git worktree with a persistent per-job target dir, so the main tree is never dirtied (linting can run concurrently) and 29 specs finish in ~40 s on 4 jobs instead of ~4 min sequential.
- A full all-KILLED run is cached in `var/bench/mutants-cache.json` (gitignored), keyed by source and specs hashes; an unchanged re-run re-reports in under a second.
- Default sequential behavior is unchanged; usage guide §5.4 documents the flags.
- SIGTERM (tool timeouts) routes through the file-restore `finally` instead of leaving a mutant applied.

## [2026-10-14] (wall clock) M8b: an accuracy benchmark with answer keys, part 2
- **Three independent keys,** so every class and every certainty label but `dispatch` (not stored) is now measured:
  - **microSecEnD:** 12 of 17 Java microservice apps pass an evidence check after pinning (`bench/pin_microsecend.py`). They score endpoints and service-to-service flows.
  - **Blind hand keys** (`bench/keys/`): one stress repo and two repos rb had never run on. They cover endpoints, handlers, table access and entity mappings, and were adjudicated by a blind adjudicator through `rb-bench statements` and `bench/adjudicate.py`.
  - **The `exact` sample:** 249 edges in 5 families judged against the source (`bench/samples/exact.tsv`), with Wilson intervals. The gate refuses a missing verdict or a new wrong `exact` edge.
- **Scoreboard** (`bench/scoreboard/m8b.json`, the same M7 binary):
  - `exact` is right on 122 of 130 independently judged edges (6 misses from unapplied config-server context paths, 2 from test-code routes), and the sample bounds every family above 86 % (`orm`, with only 9 edges, above 70 %). The certainty table counts `table_ops` rows once (`synthetic` 16 of 24) and every other label but `dispatch` (not stored) is measured.
  - microSecEnD endpoints: precision 88.7 %, recall 75.8 % after 23 removals through append-only overrides (`bench/keys/microsecend/*.md`: 6 context-path forms, 17 key errors; 2 further artifacts stay in gold as fn).
  - The 260 M8a cells are unchanged.
- **Findings B7–B14:**
  - routes in test code exposed as `exact`;
  - spec annotations overriding the router;
  - gin method-value handlers unbound;
  - `fmt.Sprintf` SQL unread;
  - the Spring Data repository-type convention (all 8 wrong `synthetic` edges);
  - Spring Data REST and service-discovery calls missed;
  - SCIP `IMPLEMENTS` from methods;
  - unapplied config-server context paths (B14).
- **Verification:**
  - Mutants m201–m229, all killed (m228 rewritten after its line gained the `table_ops` clause; m229 covers that exclusion).
  - Two scorings `cmp`-identical.
  - Real-Docker 1,122/0/0, and rb-bench's 79 tests after the last fixes.
  - The `rb` binary is unchanged.
- **Files:**
  - **Crate:** `crates/rb-bench/src/{structural,msecend,handkey,sample}.rs` (new) and `{identity,pycg,run,render,compare,scoreboard,main,lib}.rs`.
  - **Bench:** `bench/{manifest.tsv,run.sh,pin_microsecend.py,adjudicate.py}`, `bench/keys/`, `bench/samples/exact.tsv`, `bench/mutants/m8b.json`, `bench/scoreboard/m8b{,-perf}.json`.

## [2026-10-13] (wall clock) M8a: an accuracy benchmark with answer keys, part 1
- **What:** rb's first measurement against answer keys. The new `rb-bench` crate reads `rb dump` and matches edges by location (a symbol is the path and line of its name token, a file its path), so SCIP keys, heuristic keys and external keys compare. It scores every gold item and found edge into exactly one bucket, stores counts only, renders tables and gates a milestone per cell (`compare`: 0 pass, 1 regression, 2 not comparable; a new wrong `exact` edge is never excusable).
- **Keys:** SCIP agreement (Tier B against the same commit's `scip` `CALLS`, labelled agreement, not accuracy), and PyCG's 119 micro-benchmark cases (independent).
- **Committed tooling:** `bench/` (manifest of 14 pinned targets, `fetch.sh`, `run.sh` with per-run timing, disk floor and self-copy, `cleanup.sh`, mutant specs). These replace the gitignored `var/` scripts as the source of truth.
- **First scoreboard** (`bench/scoreboard/m8a.json`, M7 close binary):
  - PyCG `calls` P 92.3 % R 25.0 %, module-level P 98.9 % R 50.5 %.
  - SCIP `calls` agreement P 74.3 % R 49.7 %, dominated by mattermost.
  - Findings B1–B6 in `tasks/bench-findings.md`. The biggest: SCIP mode drops about 7,700 calls across Go modules on mattermost (6,108 of its 6,147 disagreements).
- **Docs:** the README and usage guide no longer say "`exact` only from SCIP" and name every producer of `exact`; usage guide §5.5 covers the benchmark.
- **Evaluator passes:**
  - **Pass 1:** FAIL, 2 blocking (ambiguous sites, aliases); fixed.
  - **Pass 2:** FAIL, 2 blocking (aliases unused by scoring, S4 overclaimed) and 6 should-fix; answered in amendment 4. That covers aliases in SCIP agreement, a gate rule for certainty downgrades, hardened `run.sh` (stale outputs removed, exit 1 on failure, clean-up on every exit, process-group timeout, `rb doctor` first), more real PyCG cases, and records corrected.
- **Verification:**
  - Mutants m151–m200: 49 live specs killed against the final code, m193 retired.
  - Determinism: final scoring of run 4 `cmp`-identical twice; runs 2 and 3 identical earlier.
  - Gate self-tests on the real scoreboard.

## [2026-10-13] (wall clock) M7i: precision and cost remainder (stress batch F, part 2)
- **Express case:** routes match client paths case-insensitively, as Express does, unless their router opts into `caseSensitive`; such links say `case: folded` and are never `synthetic`.
- **Specs:** `rb status` counts routes only a spec states (`18 routes (3 from specs only)`); JSON specs carry each operation's path-key span.
- **Callers of a handler:** the endpoint it handles is named after the target; an empty tree points to `rb path`.
- **Hub answers:** `callers`/`impact --limit N` (a summary first, `truncated: limit`); `callers --exclude-tests`.
- **Indexing:** one `rb index` per repository at a time (the second waits, then is usually up to date). Cached facts stream: mattermost's cached peak 2,148 → 1,658 MiB.

## [2026-10-13] (wall clock) M7h: the CLI remainder (stress batch F, part 1)
- **Checkouts:** `rb index` records each repository's checkout (migration `0007_repo_checkout`). Without `--repo`, a question, `check` or `dump` inside that checkout answers from it whatever it was named, and `rb index .` re-indexes it. Several repositories at one checkout, or a directory name already recorded at another checkout, give a directive, never a guess.
- **`find`:** `--kind` alone lists that kind; a search that matches nothing says `no matches for "x"` on stderr.
- **`service NAME --limit`** says that page flags page the service list.
- **Cypher:** writes are refused as `cypher is read-only: …`; a RETURN/`--cols` count mismatch is a `--cols` error (exit 2). A bad `RB_CYPHER_TIMEOUT_MS` is named as the variable in `cypher`, `check` and `serve`.
- **`rb doctor`:** one row per prerequisite (database, Postgres and AGE, migrations, age-load dir and mount, Docker, SCIP images, work dir, disk), each with its fix; exits 1 on a failure.
- **Docs:** README and usage guide; a stray code fence in the README removed.
- **Verification:** fixes F3, U1, U4, U7, U9. Mutants m99–m122. Real-Docker suites 1,028/0/0. Invariant vs M7g 31/31 byte-identical.
- **Files:** `migrations/0007_repo_checkout.sql`, `crates/rb-store/src/{lib,db_tests}.rs`, `crates/rb-api/src/{lib,error}.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/src/{main,doctor}.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/tests/{e2e.rs,common/mod.rs}`, `README.md`, `docs/usage-and-testing.md`; records `tasks/{contract-m7h,todo,stress-findings}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

Each target's files are one range of the sorted paths.
- **Key:** `project_input_hash(recipe, files, egress)` hashes exactly the exported files (the root filter moved to the selection); the Java pin of phase 6 holds.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M6a phase 5: workspace membership (S8; D5)
- **`rb_scip::jobs(files, read) -> Plan`:** a package root whose `package.json` declares `workspaces` (an array of strings, or Yarn's `{ "packages": [...] }`) and holds `package-lock.json` or a Yarn v1 `yarn.lock` groups the package roots strictly under it that its patterns match; the allowed forms are literal segments of `[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]` (not `.`/`..`), `*` as a whole segment and `**` as the whole final segment, with a trailing `/` ignored, compiled into a trie walked once per candidate. Anything else leaves the root ungrouped with a reason (`workspace_glob_unsupported`, `workspace_without_lockfile`, `workspace_lockfile_unsupported` for a pnpm lockfile, `yarn_berry`, `manifest_unreadable`) and the jobs as before. Roots are visited parents first, so an outer workspace claims a nested root first and a member's own `workspaces` and lockfile are not read. The grouped root's job indexes every `tsconfig.json` directory whose nearest package root is the root or a member; `nearest_root` walks up the directories through a set (linear).
- **Recorded:** `Plan::members` per grouped root → `ScipProject::workspace_members` → `scip_status` `workspace_members` (top level, only when non-zero) → `rb status` `, workspace <k> members`; `Plan::ungrouped` → `Indexed::scip_ungrouped` → `rb index` prints `scip: workspace <root> not grouped (<reason>)`, nothing stored (amendment 5).
- **Tests (new):** `rb-scip` `a_workspace_root_with_a_lockfile_groups_its_members` (npm array, Yarn object, literal, `*`, `**` at depth ≥ 1, trailing `/`, an unmatched extra pattern; `legacy` keeps its job), `an_unsupported_pattern_leaves_the_workspace_ungrouped` (14 patterns, 4 manifest shapes), `a_workspace_without_a_supported_lockfile_is_ungrouped` (`polyglot-monorepo`'s shape, Yarn berry, pnpm, Yarn v1), `members_lockfiles_and_nested_roots`, `workspace_membership_grows_linearly` (S19); `rb-indexer` `workspace_members_are_written_only_for_a_grouped_root`; CLI status-line row. The existing job units keep their expectations (one comment updated).
- **Mutations:** a partial `*` accepted → killed by the unsupported-pattern unit; grouping without a lockfile → killed by the `polyglot-monorepo` row; a scan of every literal child per trie node → killed by the growth unit. Each restored and byte-compared.
- **Invariant 1:** the three fixtures' dumps with and without SCIP byte-identical to `var/m6a/base`; `polyglot-monorepo`'s root is reported `not grouped (workspace_without_lockfile)` by `rb index` only.
- **Verification** (`var/m6a/logs/p5-*`): fmt clean; clippy failed in the loop on two test statements (`run_projects(…).unwrap().0;`, `unnecessary_operation`), fixed and re-checked (clippy clean, `projects` units pass); 662 unit tests outside e2e and api; `rb-store` 66/66, `rb-server` 12/12; e2e 31/31, api 1/1, no `skipping`; no listener; stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: `crates/rb-scip/{src/jobs.rs,Cargo.toml}`, `crates/rb-indexer/src/{projects,lib}.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/src/main.rs`, `Cargo.lock` (rb-scip's dependency list); records `tasks/{contract-m6a,todo}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M6a phase 4: TypeScript interface-method `IMPLEMENTS` (S5–S7; D4; `PIPELINE` `m6a-2`)
- **Gate:** P1b (phase 0) found no super→sub member relationship over 13 forms, so D4 is built.
- **Join:** when the type holder of a relationship's source is an interface in a TypeScript, TSX or JavaScript document, the `IMPLEMENTS` walk steps onto its declared supertypes (each resolved through the index's own type occurrence at the written name, amendment 26) without the origin's own relations, which scip-typescript leaves empty for interfaces; the relationship itself must still come from the source's own symbol information, and the walk must reach the target's type. Java interfaces and every class keep amendment 78's confirmation. `supertypes_unconfirmed` is unchanged (it already excluded TypeScript interfaces).
- **Tests (new):** `typescript_interface_methods_implement_what_they_override` (one level, two clauses, depth 3 with both overridden members, a generic super-interface), `typescript_interface_methods_implement_imported_and_qualified_supertypes` (another file; `extends ns.Base`), `typescript_interface_links_the_clauses_do_not_confirm_give_no_edge` (a fabricated `Mid#m() → Unrelated#m()`, the reverse `Base#m() → Mid#m()`, a property signature), `a_java_interface_keeps_its_origin_confirmation`, `a_typescript_class_keeps_its_origin_confirmation`. The two positive units failed first (no edge on `cfa8ab9`'s walk).
- **Mutations:** waiver removed → the two positive units; waiver on Java interfaces → survived the amendment-78 unit S5 named (its types are classes), killed by `a_java_interface_keeps_its_origin_confirmation`; waiver on every TypeScript type → killed by `a_typescript_class_keeps_its_origin_confirmation` (amendment 4). ## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M6a phase 2: Lombok-generated occurrences removed before the agreement checks (S1, S2, S3; D1–D3; `PIPELINE` `m6a-1`)
- **`rb_scip::symbol::is_lombok_generated_marker`:** `@lombok.Generated` by the parsed symbol: package `maven/org.projectlombok/lombok` (any version), descriptors exactly `lombok/` and `Generated#`.
- **`rb-indexer` join:** for a scip-java index, each document's occurrences at a marker's span and its definitions (global or local) enclosed exactly by a marker's span are removed before the decoder's reasons, `definition_disagreement`, `definitions_unpaired` and `structure_disagreement` run, all unchanged; a document without a marker is used as it is. Counted for accepted documents: `lombok_removed`, `documents_lombok`, written to the `scip_status` coverage only when non-zero; `rb status` prints `, lombok-generated <n> in <d> documents` after the rejected documents, only when non-zero. Operation counts: one pass into a sorted set, one filtering pass.
- **Tests (new):** `rb-scip` `the_lombok_marker_is_identified_by_package_and_descriptors`; `rb-indexer` `a_data_class_is_accepted_once_its_generated_members_are_removed` (S1 a, c, d, against a twin index without Lombok), `a_field_getter_defined_at_a_call_is_removed_and_its_call_is_undocumented` (b), `a_builder_and_its_nested_type_are_removed` (e), `a_hand_written_generated_method_is_kept` (f), `an_anonymous_class_inside_a_marker_span_is_kept` (g), `an_enum_constant_body_still_binds_in_a_lombok_enum` (h), `lombok_removal_never_clears_another_verdict` (j, k), `a_hand_written_method_at_a_generated_definitions_position_keeps_its_own_key` (S2, P2b's positions), `lombok_removal_grows_linearly` (S19), `lombok_counters_are_written_only_when_non_zero` (S3); CLI `scip_status` unit gains the Lombok line. Every position is derived from the source. Before the change 7 of the join units failed (a, b, e, f's counters, g, h, S2: rejected or no counter).

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 evaluator pass 1 fixed: unknown repos, self-healing graphs, Host strictness (amendment 16)
- **Evaluator pass 1** (`tasks/eval-m5-pass1.md`, against `9a2719f`, VERDICT FAIL) fixed test-first, as the orchestrator decided.
- **Z1:** `status` of a repository never indexed is `not_found` (exit 3 / `404`) on both surfaces, checked in `rb-api`.
- **Z2:** a publisher killed after its flip, before its retention, left the old snapshot's AGE graph and Cypher answered on the retired snapshot. Reads now decide from the status row: `Store::cypher` reads a graph only for a `live` row (`invalid_param` otherwise, D17), and `SnapshotInfo` names a graph only for a `live` row. The window heals itself: retention drops every non-live graph of its repository (shared `drop_non_live_graphs`), and `rb serve` sweeps every repository before it listens (`Store::sweep_graphs`, one graph per transaction under the DDL lock; a sweep that cannot run is a stderr warning).
- **Z3:** the server percent-decodes each path segment once, after splitting (`%5F` = `_`, `%2F` stays in the segment, malformed escapes `invalid_param`).
- **Z4:** two `Host` headers → `400`; an absolute-form authority off the allow-list → `421`, an allowed one that differs from `Host` → `400`.
- **Z5, Z6:** API rows request a `failed` snapshot (`not_found`); retention with two and three non-live graphs is tested, and the retention property test gains the stopped publish.
- **Z7 (residue):** hyper's own refusals (`414`, `431`, `400`) carry no body and come from its protocol layer before any service, so no JSON is possible cheaply; recorded in the soundness table and open item 23, pinned by a test.
- **Open items:** 22 (adversarial egress review carried to M6), 23 (Z7); Z2's crash window recorded as closed for answers.
- **Tests (new):** `rb-store` `a_publish_stopped_after_its_flip_leaves_a_graph_that_reads_ignore_and_sweeps_remove` (test-only hook `with_stop_after_flip`), `retention_holds_over_random_sequences` with a fourth operation; `rb-server` `status_of_a_repository_never_indexed_is_not_found`, `path_segments_are_percent_decoded_once`, `host_headers_and_absolute_form_are_strict`, `transport_refusals_are_hyper_bare_statuses`; e2e `a_publish_killed_after_its_flip_self_heals` (the evaluator's lock + `SIGKILL` repro, no hook); API identity rows for a never-indexed `status`/`snapshots`, encoded and unicode repository segments, and a `failed` snapshot through `callers`, `find`, `cypher`. `postgres` is now an `rb-cli` dev-dependency (already in the lockfile; no new crate).
- **Mutations:** 16 of 16 killed (m16, m17, Z1's check, Cypher's `live` gate, the `SnapshotInfo` mask, the serve sweep call, decoding removed, decoding twice, hex in base 36, duplicate-`Host` at `> 2`, the authority allow-list, authority = `Host`; Z1, the gate, the mask and the decoding also through the e2e or API suites); each file restored from its copy and byte-compared.
- **Invariant 1:** the three fixture dumps equal phase 20's (`var/m5/p20/dumps/`) byte for byte.
- **Housekeeping:** `rb cache prune --facts` removed 19,783 stale facts rows (649,322,998 bytes); 19,123 current rows kept.
- **Verification** (`var/m5/logs/ev1-*`): fmt, clippy clean; 639 unit tests outside e2e and api; `rb-store` with the database 65/65, `rb-server` 12/12; e2e 30/30 (276.5 s), api 1/1 (150 parameter sets identical plus the new rows; statuses 200 ×100, 400 ×21, 404 ×18, 409 ×8, 410 ×1, 422 ×3), no `skipping`; no `rb` listener; stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 17a: publish analyzes its tables (amendment 14); shardingsphere indexing and storage (S24)
- **Found by the benchmark:** a read of a snapshot right after its publish (`rb dump`, any CLI question, `rb serve`'s index build after the pointer moves) could take a plan quadratic in the snapshot's size: until autovacuum's next analyze (up to a minute) the statistics predate the COPY, the planner estimated 1 row for the new `snapshot_id` and joined `rb.edge_evidence` to `rb.node` as a nested loop over every node. On shardingsphere a dump did not finish in 10 minutes (seen three times, once with the `afae548` binary); with fresh statistics it takes about 1 s.
- **Fix (`rb-store` `load_and_flip`):** `ANALYZE rb.node, rb.edge, rb.edge_evidence, rb.diagnostic` as the publish transaction's last statement: the statistics count the transaction's own rows and commit with the flip. Cost 0.7–1.6 s per publish on shardingsphere. No dump changes.
- **Test (failed first):** `a_publish_leaves_statistics_that_cover_the_new_snapshot` (scratch database; each table's estimate for the new snapshot at least half its rows; before: `rb.node: 1 rows estimated …, 800 present`). Mutation: `rb.edge_evidence` left out of the ANALYZE → killed.
- **S24, apache/shardingsphere** (contract "Benchmark measurements", `var/m5/bench/out/ss-*`): `--no-scip` fresh 35.0 s (1.26 GiB), cached 23.4 s, incremental 18.5 s and 19.2 s; SCIP fresh 1,878.9 s (fetch ≈ 641 s, scip-java index phase ≈ 1,086 s on its 2-CPU budget; ≈ 1,238 s without the fetch: §12.5's 15 min **missed**, profile: 96.6 % of `rb`'s main thread waits on the one container), cached 49.5 s (2.38 GiB); fresh == cached (both modes), empty self-diffs, full AGE parity; one snapshot 173 MiB relational + 64 MiB AGE; retention's delete 1.40 s; facts cache +9,851 rows (≈ 9 % of the repository's snapshots: no D13 gap); scip-java documents rejected 3,889 (3,771 `structure_disagreement`; Lombok suspected, M6); no stage superlinear between half and whole; S18/S19 change nothing there (byte-identical to `afae548`). A SCIP incremental re-indexes the whole root project; not run twice (disk: the fresh run took free space from 14.1 to 8.3 GiB).
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 634 unit tests outside e2e and api; `rb-store` with the database 64/64, `rb-server` 8/8; e2e 29/29, api 1/1 (150 sets), no `skipping`; no `rb` listener; stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: `crates/rb-store/src/{lib,db_tests}.rs`, `tasks/{contract-m5,todo,lessons}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 15: fetch-phase egress and per-repository caches (S21, S22)
- **Proxy image** (`docker/egress-proxy/`, F2's constraint): the local Alpine 3.24.2 base by digest plus `tinyproxy=1.11.3-r0`, run as `tinyproxy`; built `rb-egress-proxy:1.11.3`, ID `sha256:9e2f31c4e334fb7870d4cdbc86f82d86c22dc7a6c0730884d2acdc09dd62c035`, 13,831,923 bytes. No image was pulled.
- **`rb_scip::egress`** (new): `Egress` (the built-in allow-list `repo.maven.apache.org`, `repo1.maven.org`, `registry.npmjs.org`, `registry.yarnpkg.com` plus validated extra hosts), the tinyproxy configuration (`FilterDefaultDeny Yes`, `FilterURLs On`, `FilterType fnmatch`, `ConnectPort 443`, `LogLevel Connect`) and filter (`host:443` lines), Maven's `settings.xml`, the proxy environment for npm/Yarn/pnpm, and the refusals read from the proxy's log.
- **Runner:** `Network::Fetch` replaces `Registries` (`--network rb-scip-fetch --dns 127.0.0.1`; the default bridge is never used); `ContainerRun::readonly` mounts; `Runner::start_proxy` (create the `--internal` fetch network and the egress bridge when absent, labelled `rb.egress`; refuse an existing non-internal `rb-scip-fetch`; start the proxy on the bridge with its configuration read-only, connect it to the fetch network, wait until it listens, return its address) and `stop_proxy` (its log, then removal); `DockerRunner::egress_leftovers` for `rb gc --egress`.
- **`run_job`:** one proxy per run (`rb-egress-<run instance>`, amendment 12), started before the first fetch and stopped before the index phase whatever happened; fetch containers get the proxy environment and `-s /rb-egress/settings.xml`; a proxy that cannot start is `environment` (not cached) and nothing runs; a failed fetch reports `egress proxy refused: …`; without the proxy image `Images::for_job` makes every job `unsupported`. `project_input_hash` takes the allow-list. `RECIPE` `m5-egress-1`.
- **S22:** `rb index` keys the package caches by repository (`<scip-work-dir>/cache/<repo>`); both Maven phases turn the Develocity and Gradle Enterprise build caches off; `npm_config_update_notifier=false` (a fresh per-repository cache made npm print an update notice into a failed fetch's stored stderr on the first run only; lesson recorded).

- **Measured:** the fixtures' fetches reach only `repo.maven.apache.org` and `registry.npmjs.org` (through an open proxy). Probe suite: allow-listed registry 200; another host, an IP through the proxy and plain HTTP refused (403) and logged; direct TCP outside and to the host gateway, DNS and UDP all fail; the fetch network is internal. spring-retry @ `40f557cc27ab`: files without a document 68 → 0 (definitions 724 → 1,618; `unresolved_references` 58 → 0), fresh == cached and cold == warm cache byte for byte, the shared caches untouched. Separate caches cost spring-retry 184 MiB and the fixtures 4.3/282/284 MiB.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 11: `rb diff`, exact sections (S16)
- **New module `rb_model::diff`** (pure; contract M5 D11): `NodeRow` and `EdgeRow` are a graph's rows as the canonical dump writes them, with their identities (`(kind, key)`; `(src kind, src key, kind, dst kind, dst key, provenance)`) and their `N`/`E` lines; `node_rows`/`edge_rows` list them in the dump's order. `diff(a, b) -> GraphDiff`: a merge-join of the two sorted row lists giving `nodes_added`, `nodes_removed`, `nodes_changed` (name or attributes; before and after), `edges_added`, `edges_removed`, `edges_changed` (certainty or attributes, the evidence possibly too), `edges_evidence_only`; every section in the dump's order; no move inference. `GraphDiff::invert`; `apply(lines, diff)`, a checked patch of a dump's `N`/`E` lines (a removed or changed row must be present exactly as its before-row renders, an added one absent; the result in the dump's order); `diagnostics_diff` (count per kind before and after; the multiset difference of the `D` lines, canonical order).
- **`canonical_dump`** now renders its rows through `node_rows`/`edge_rows`/`diagnostic_line`, so the diff and the dump share one rendering; every fixture dump is byte-identical to `afae548` and the `dump.jsonl` golden is unchanged.
- **Tests:** `each_section_holds_its_row` (a node added/removed, an attrs-only node change, a certainty change, attributes with evidence, evidence only, an edge removed; each applied), `apply_checks_what_it_patches`, `diagnostics_differ_as_multisets`, `diff_applies_inverts_and_is_empty_on_itself` (200 seeded pairs of random graphs from one candidate universe: every node and edge kind, three provenances, self-loops and cycles, duplicate names; `apply(A, diff(A, B)) == B` line for line and back with the inverse, `diff(A, A)` empty, `diff(B, A) == invert(diff(A, B))`; every section non-empty somewhere), `diff_grows_linearly` (counted sort and merge steps: 5,916 → 23,770 for 3,549 → 14,227 rows, 4.0×; bound 5×).
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p11.json`): 5/5 killed (evidence-only folded into changed, `invert` keeping the changes, `apply` unchecked, node attributes ignored, a quadratic merge).
- **Invariant 1:** fixture dumps byte-identical to `afae548`.
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean (one `collapsible_if` fixed after the first run); 613 unit tests outside e2e and api, `rb-store` with the database 62/62, `rb-server` 8/8; e2e 25/25, api 1/1 (no `skipping`); no `rb` listener; stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: new `crates/rb-model/src/diff.rs`, new `crates/rb-model/src/diff/tests.rs`, `crates/rb-model/src/{lib,graph}.rs`, `tasks/todo.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 9: `rb serve` (S13; D1, D3, D7, D8)
- **New crate `rb-server`, command `rb serve [--bind 127.0.0.1:55480] [--workers 4] [--queue 16] [--deadline-ms 10000] [--cypher-timeout-ms 2000]`.** Endpoints under `/v1`: `GET /health`, `GET /repos` (every repository's status), `GET /repos/{repo}/{status,snapshots,find,services,service,callers,impact,table-deps,path}`, `POST /repos/{repo}/cypher` (`{"query", "columns"?, "timeout_ms"?}`); parameters in the query string; each answer is `rb-api`'s body, the bytes `rb … --json` prints. It prints `rb serve: listening on http://<addr>` and stops on SIGINT/SIGTERM after the requests in flight.
- **Guard rails, each tested:** a non-loopback `--bind` is refused at startup; a `Host` other than the bound loopback address or `localhost` with the bound port (or none) → `421 misdirected_request`; no `Access-Control-*` header on any response, `OPTIONS` and every other method an endpoint does not take → `405 method_not_allowed` with `Allow`; Cypher's `POST` without `Content-Type: application/json` → `415 unsupported_media_type`; an unknown or repeated parameter, a value out of bounds or an unknown Cypher body field → `400 invalid_param`, unknown path → `404`; these come before any database work. Then a slot: `--workers` run, `--queue` wait, one more gets `503 busy` at once; the work runs in `spawn_blocking` holding its slot and a pooled read-only session (`Store::connect_read_only`); past `--deadline-ms` (from arrival) → `503 deadline_exceeded` without a result, the abandoned work keeping its slot; timings only in `Server-Timing` (`queue`, `run`, `total`).
- **Index cache (D3):** each repository's live snapshot's `SnapshotIndex` and one other snapshot's, shared by the workers; a request after the live pointer moved builds and caches the new one (running requests keep their `Arc`).
- **Dependencies (D1, amendment 7):** axum 0.8.9 (`http1`, `tokio`, `query`; no default features) and tokio's `rt-multi-thread`, `net`, `signal`, `time`, `sync`. 21 external crates enter `Cargo.lock` (287 → 309 packages with `rb-server`), all MIT, Apache-2.0, or MIT AND BSD-3-Clause (matchit); release `rb` 22,639,808 → 23,869,248 bytes (+1.17 MiB). Under the guard (30 crates, 3 MiB): no question to the orchestrator.
- **`rb-api`:** server-only error kinds `misdirected_request` (421), `method_not_allowed` (405), `unsupported_media_type` (415). **`rb-store`:** `connect_read_only`, `session_read_only`.
- **Found while testing:** dropping the pooled synchronous postgres connections inside the server's runtime panicked at shutdown (`Cannot start a runtime from within a runtime`); they are now dropped after the runtime.
- **Tests:** `rb-server` units `a_non_loopback_bind_is_refused`, `a_foreign_host_is_misdirected`, `methods_and_media_types_are_allow_listed`, `parameters_and_paths_are_checked_first`, `a_full_queue_is_busy_at_once` (1 worker, queue 1, held work: the third request is `busy` in under a second), `a_deadline_is_an_error_never_a_partial_body`, `cypher_bodies_become_parameters` (a raw HTTP/1.1 client checks every reply for CORS headers and `Server-Timing`), `pooled_connections_are_read_only_sessions` (database); `rb-store` `read_only_sessions_refuse_writes` (database); e2e `serve_answers_from_the_live_snapshot` (a real `rb serve` on an ephemeral port: the body equals `rb callers --json`, a new commit indexed under the running server is answered from the new snapshot, a Cypher `CREATE` → 422 read-only, SIGINT exits cleanly). `var/m5/scripts/verify.sh` now runs `rb-server` with the database and checks that no `rb` listener is left.
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p9.json`): 8/8 killed (Host unchecked, any bind, `OPTIONS` allowed, any media type, the queue one longer, no deadline, plain pooled connections, a stale live index).
- **Invariant 1:** fixture dumps byte-identical to `afae548`.
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 608 unit tests outside e2e, `rb-store` with the database 62/62, `rb-server` with the database 8/8; e2e 25/25 (`--nocapture`, no `skipping`); no `rb` listener left; stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, new `crates/rb-server/{Cargo.toml,src/lib.rs,src/tests.rs}`, `crates/rb-cli/{Cargo.toml,src/main.rs,tests/e2e.rs}`, `crates/rb-api/src/error.rs`, `crates/rb-store/src/{lib,db_tests}.rs`, `tasks/{contract-m5,todo}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 8: pagination (S15, D18)
- **Flat lists page:** `find` (`limit` default 20, max 1,000), `services` and `snapshots` (default and max 1,000) take `limit` and `offset` (0 to 2^63−1) and answer `{"total", "offset", "limit", "next_offset", "items"}`, `next_offset` `null` on the last page and past the end. `Store::find` runs one `LIMIT/OFFSET` query plus one count and returns `FoundPage { nodes, total }`; in-memory lists are paged by `rb_api::Paged::of`, which takes at most `offset + limit` elements from the list's iterator. Every order is total (find ends on kind and key bytewise, services by key, snapshots by id), and snapshots are immutable, so pages never drift.
- **CLI:** `rb find --offset`, `rb service --limit --offset` (the list; a card takes neither: `invalid_param`), `rb snapshots --limit --offset`. Text prints the page's rows on stdout and, when more remain, `rows a-b of n listed; next page: --offset m` on stderr, so stdout stays rows only and no text golden changes.
- **Golden (classified):** `polyglot-monorepo/find-auth.json`: `result` was the array of matches and is now the page object; its `items` equal the old array exactly, the page says `total: 25, offset: 0, limit: 8, next_offset: 8`, and the query gains `offset: 0` (script: `var/m5/logs/p8-golden-classify.log`).
- **Tests:** `rb-api` units `pages_concatenate_to_the_whole_list` (lists of 0 to 1,001 rows at limits 1, 7, 1,000; `total`; `next_offset` null exactly at the end; past the end), `a_page_examines_at_most_offset_plus_limit_elements` (counted, n up to 100,000), `page_parameters_are_bounded`; database `find_pages_concatenate_to_the_whole_list`; CLI `more_line_names_the_next_offset`; e2e `flat_lists_page_by_limit_and_offset` (find, services, snapshots at limits 1, 7, 1,000; past the end; the stderr line).
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p8*.json`): 6/6 killed (a page reading the whole list, `next_offset` past the end, `find` ignoring the offset, the count ignoring the kind, the CLI hint dropped, the CLI dropping `--offset`). The last one first hung the e2e page loop, which followed `next_offset` without a bound; the harness was stopped with SIGINT and the source checked restored; the loop now asserts each page starts where asked, that the next offset grows, and stops after `total` pages (lesson 2026-10-07).
- **Invariant 1:** fixture dumps byte-identical to `afae548`.
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 599 unit tests outside e2e, `rb-store` with the database 61/61; e2e 24/24 (`--nocapture`, no `skipping`); stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: `crates/rb-api/src/lib.rs`, `crates/rb-store/src/{query,lib,db_tests}.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/src/main.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/tests/e2e.rs`, one golden, `tasks/{todo,lessons}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 7b: JSON everywhere and `--snapshot` (S11, D17)
- **Envelope (D4) on every `--json`:** `{"api": 1, "repo", "snapshot": {"id", "commit_sha"}, "query" (normalised, defaults filled), "limits" (caps in force), "truncated", "certainty" (when rows carry one), "result"}`. With `--json` a refused request prints its error body on stdout and exits with D5's code.
- **`--snapshot <id>` on every question (D17):** a kept snapshot of the repository (live or retired) answers from its rows; a retired one answers byte-for-byte as it did while live; deleted → `snapshot_not_retained` (exit 3, HTTP 410); another repository's, a `failed` one or an id never issued → `not_found`; Cypher on a retired one → `invalid_param` (no projection). `rb-store`: `SnapshotInfo.parent_id`, `Store::snapshot_opt`, `snapshots`, `snapshot_issued`; `ResolutionCoverage` serialises; `work::metered`/`Spent` public.

- **`RB_TEST_CAPS`** (S14's test-only override): lowers `nodes`, `tree`, `work` for one process; raising is refused; shown in `limits`.

Logs `var/m5/logs/hk-*.log`.
- **New crate `rb-api`** (no HTTP; contract M5 D2): `error` (D5: `ErrorKind` with exit code and HTTP status, `ApiError { kind, message, param, candidates }`, its body `{"api": 1, "error": {…}}` and its CLI text, which names the flag of an `invalid_param`), `params` (the string map: unknown and duplicate names refused, integers within D6's bounds and never clamped, booleans `true`/`false`, certainties, precisions, node kinds), `render` (moved from `rb-cli` without behaviour change: `pick_target`, `pick_endpoint`, `describe_line`, `endpoint_service`, `node_json`, `impact_json`, the request-path JSON and `PathView`; new: `callers_json`), `lib` (`Endpoint`, `parse` → `Request`/`Query` with the normalised query, `run`: the repository's live snapshot and every read in one `REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY` transaction, Cypher in its own after it; `Answer`, `Output::body` — the D4 envelope, printed from phase 7b — and `result_json`; `Indexes` with `LoadEach` for the CLI).
- **CLI:** `find`, `service`, `table-deps`, `callers`, `impact`, `path` and `cypher` hand their flags to `rb-api` as text (`asked`), which alone validates them; text is rendered from the same `Answer`; `--json`/`--format json` print the bare result as before. A refused request prints `Error: <message>` and exits with D5's code (2 invalid parameter, 3 not found / no live snapshot, 4 ambiguous, 5 Cypher). `main` returns an `ExitCode`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 6b: guard rails complete (S10)
- **D6 in one table** (`rb_store::limits`): caller and path depths, hops, `NODES` 200,000, `TREE` 5,000, `WORK` 20,000,000, `ROWS` 10,000, the Cypher timeouts; `Limit` (`depth`, `hops`, `nodes`, `tree`, `work`, `rows`) and `Caps` (`nodes`, `tree`, `work`). The older constants are aliases of the table.
- **Work budget:** `work::metered(cap, f)` runs a question with a per-request budget (a nested question, Q3's API hops, joins it); every adjacency element a `View` reads is `spend`-ed, and once the budget is spent further elements are refused and the answer names `work`. Exact: a budget of n admits n elements. Counted in every build (`tick` was test-only).
- **Q1:** `tree` (caller entries, 5,000) and `work`; `CallerTree.truncated`, `.work`; `CallerOptions.caps`. **Q2:** `Impact.truncated` is a set of names (`nodes`, `work`; was `bool`), `nodes` checked wherever a node is reached, orphans `not computed (work cap reached)` after a `work` cut, `impact_capped` takes `Caps`; its sort keys are computed once per element so the work count does not depend on hash-map order. **Q4:** the answer-node cap is `tree` (was `nodes`), plus `work`; `PathOptions.caps`. **Q3:** `nodes` and `work`; `RollupOptions.caps`; `work` is not serialised.
- **CLI:** `truncated: <limits>` after `rb callers` and `rb impact` text (was `truncated: stopped after 200000 nodes`); `rb impact --json` lists the names.
- **Tests:** `capped_answers_name_every_cap_that_cut_them` (seeded random graphs with cycles over the real kinds; Q1–Q4; caps raised: untruncated and equal to the default-cap run; each cap lowered to every value: a differing answer names it, an untruncated one equals the uncapped; `tree` for Q1 and `work` everywhere strict), `each_cap_hits_at_one_below_its_need`, `rollup_caps_hit_at_one_below_their_need`, the Q4 tree row at 6,001/6,000, `work_counts_are_deterministic`, `a_budget_admits_exactly_its_cap`, `truncated_lines_name_the_limits`, and the e2e `work_cap_is_far_above_every_fixture_question` (calibration: largest default question Q1 12, Q2 489, Q4 178, Q3 92 operations; contract amendment 4).
- **Golden (classified):** `java-spring-jpa/impact-legacy-orphans.json`: `"truncated": false` → `"truncated": []`, S10's named change; blessed alone after reading the diff.
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p6b*.json`): 13/13 killed; the Q3 nodes-cap mutant survived the property test (an uncapped answer is complete) and is killed by the boundary row added for it.
- **Invariant 1:** fixture dumps byte-identical to `afae548`.
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 582 unit tests outside e2e (10 database tests vacuous without the URL), `rb-store` with the database 59/59; e2e 22/22 (`--nocapture`, no `skipping`); stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 6a: prebuilt snapshot index (S9)
- **`SnapshotIndex`** (`rb_store::code`): built once per snapshot from its `Graph` (owned): node id → position, and inbound and outbound adjacency as CSR arrays of edge indices, each list in the graph's edge order (as every question's own `HashMap` adjacency was); immutable and `Send + Sync`; its build counts `nodes + edges` operations. `View` is now the per-question state over it: the index, the precision and the pruned dispatch steps.
- **Signatures:** `resolve_target`, `Describer::new`, `callers`, `impact`, `path::resolve_endpoints`, `request_path`, `upstream_of`, `rollup::table_rollup` and `card::card_sections` take `&SnapshotIndex`; `Store::table_deps(snapshot, ix, key, opts)` and `Store::service_card(snapshot, ix, key)` no longer load the graph themselves. The CLI loads the graph in its read transaction and builds one index per invocation; its renderers take the index.
- **Tests:** `a_depth_one_question_does_not_grow_with_the_graph` (counted: failed first at 3,009 → 12,009 operations for chains of 1,000 and 4,000, now equal; the build ≤ 5× for 4×), `adjacency_keeps_the_edge_order`, `the_index_is_send_and_sync`; existing tests now build an index per graph (`ix(&g)`).

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 5: AGE projection parity and `ops` (S6)
- **Projection:** edges whose relational attributes hold `ops` (`QUERIES`, `MIGRATES`) carry it as `ops` (the same array; contract amendment 3: there is no `op` attribute), loaded from separate `<LABEL>.ops.csv` files so no other edge gets the property. Vertices load with `nid` as the loader's id column: before, every vertex also carried a redundant `id` property; AGE's own `__id__` (= `nid`) remains. `age::project` returns a `ProjectionProfile` (per file: label, rows, CSV bytes, load time), exposed as `Published.projection`.
- **`rb index --profile`:** prints `profile: age <label> (<file>): n rows, b bytes, t ms` per loaded file and the totals (wall times printed only, never stored).
- **e2e parity (`age_parity_failures`, every fixture):** the whole vertex set `(label, properties(n))` and the whole edge multiset `(label, start nid, end nid, properties(e))`, property maps compared as typed JSON with no extra property allowed; replaces the edge check without provenance and the endpoint-only vertex check. It failed first on `QUERIES` without `ops`, then on the extra `id`/`__id__` vertex properties (amendment 3).
- **Tests:** the parity check; `projection_lines_list_files_and_totals` (CLI unit).
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p5.json`): 5/5 killed (`CALLS` edges not loaded, `File` vertices not loaded, `certainty` written as text, `ops` dropped, `nid` as text).

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 4: Cypher guard rails and e2e robustness (S7, S8)
- **Migration `0006_reader_role.sql`:** role `rb_reader` (`NOLOGIN`, created only if absent), `USAGE` on `rb` and `ag_catalog`, `SELECT` on `rb.*` (and future `rb` tables), `ag_catalog.ag_graph`, `ag_catalog.ag_label`, and on every existing `rb_*` graph schema. Idempotent (applied twice in one transaction without error; `rb migrate` reruns report `schema up to date`). No table changes.
- **Publish:** the transaction grants the new graph's schema to `rb_reader`.
- **`Store::cypher`:** one `REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY` transaction; the snapshot's graph read inside it; `SET LOCAL statement_timeout` from `CypherLimits.timeout_ms` (default 2,000, at most 30,000) and `SET LOCAL ROLE rb_reader`; at most `rows + 1` fetched, more → the first 10,000 and `truncated: ["rows"]`. Refusals are `CypherError`: `NoProjection` (a retired snapshot, D10/D17), `Timeout { limit_ms }` (the message names the limit and the flag), `Refused` (the server's message: syntax, read-only, privilege).
- **`rb cypher --timeout-ms`** (1-30,000, env `RB_CYPHER_TIMEOUT_MS`); prints `truncated: rows` after the rows when cut.
- **e2e (S8):** once per run, under the mutex, the `t_*` repositories killed runs left and any `rb_t_*` graph no live snapshot names are removed through `gc_plan`/`gc_apply`; the parity and per-service Cypher queries pass `--timeout-ms 30000`.
- **Tests:** e2e `cypher_guard_rails` (timeout by flag and by environment variable, each also bounded at 10 s; `0` and `30001` refused; `CREATE` read-only; `pg_read_file('…'::pg_text)` and `pg_current_logfile()` denied; 10,001 rows → 10,000 and `truncated: rows`, 10,000 → none); database unit `cypher_on_a_retired_snapshot_is_refused`.
- **Found while probing (amendment 2):** with AGE's `::pg_text` cast, Cypher reaches `pg_read_file` — as the superuser it read a server file and leaked it in an error message; under the new role it is denied.
- **S8 measurement:** e2e three times in a row under 8 CPU burners (load up to 28.95): 21/21 each, no `skipping`; the planted leftover repository and orphan graph were removed by the first run's start-up.
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p4.json`, final run `var/m5/logs/p4-mut-final.log`): 8/8 killed (no role, no graph grants, timeout ignored, row cap fetching only the cap, truncation at the cap, read-write transaction, timeout unclassified, retired snapshot not refused). The timeout-ignored mutant survived the first version of the row (message only) and is killed by the elapsed bound.
- **Invariant 1:** fixture dumps byte-identical to `afae548`; no golden changed.
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 572 unit tests outside e2e (10 database tests vacuous without the URL), `rb-store` with the database 51/51; e2e 21/21 (`--nocapture`, no `skipping`); stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 3: `rb gc`, `rb cache prune`, `--no-cache` writes (S4, S5)
- **`rb gc [--dry-run] [--prefix P]`** (`Store::gc_plan`, `Store::gc_apply`): lists and removes AGE graphs named `rb_*` (or `rb_<P>…`) that no live snapshot names — orphans and graphs retired snapshots kept before M5 — one per transaction (repository row, then the DDL lock, then a re-check that no live snapshot names it), and `building` rows (re-checked still `building`). Never implicit. `--egress` comes with phase 15.
- **`rb cache prune --facts|--scip [--dry-run]`** (`Store::prune_facts`, `Store::prune_scip`): facts rows whose extractor is not `rb_syntax::extractor_id()` (prefix match up to the `;` before the language), SCIP rows whose image is neither the local scip-java image ID nor the pinned scip-typescript reference; prints rows and bytes; `--scip` refuses when Docker or the scip-java image cannot be identified, and an empty image list is refused.
- **`--no-cache`** reads no cached facts but writes what it extracts (`WriteOnlyFactsCache`, M4a open item 4); help text and README say so. README also documents `--keep`, the snapshot lifecycle, `rb gc` and `rb cache prune`.
- **Tests:** e2e `no_cache_writes_the_facts_it_extracted` (sources with a per-run token; failed first: the plain run parsed 3 of 3, now 0; dumps equal) and `gc_and_cache_prune_commands` (scoped `rb gc` lists nothing beside a live graph; prune dry run output; `--facts`/`--scip` required); database units `gc_removes_stale_rows_and_graphs_but_never_the_live_graph` (planted orphan graph, retired snapshot's graph, `building` row; dry run changes nothing; a stale plan naming the live graph or a non-`building` row removes nothing) and `cache_prune_keeps_the_current_rows` (in a scratch database created and dropped by the test); CLI units for the write-only cache and `rb gc`'s lines. The gc test first failed on a real bug: an orphan's `NULL` snapshot id was not `DISTINCT FROM` a `NULL` pointer, so orphans were never listed.
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p3.json`): 9/9 killed.
- **Shared database, dry runs only:** `rb gc --dry-run` would drop `rb_fp3_class_validator_274` and `rb_fp3_petclinic_272` (retired `fp3_*` snapshots' graphs kept by the pre-M5 retention) — left alone and reported; `rb cache prune --facts --scip --dry-run`: 22,913 facts rows (248,136,074 bytes), 0 SCIP rows.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 2: retention and the graph lifecycle (S3)
- **Publish:** the snapshot row is inserted inside the publish transaction, after the repository row lock (the parent is read under the lock); a failed publish rolls back everything and then records a separate `failed` row with the attempt's commit and tools; a publisher that dies before its commit leaves no row. `publish_snapshot` returns `Published { snapshot, retention }`.
- **Retention** (`Store::retain`, `Store::apply_retention`): every non-live snapshot's AGE graph is dropped in a transaction of its own (repository row lock, then `RB_AGE_DDL_LOCK`, then `age_graph = NULL`), so after a publish exactly the live snapshot has a graph (fork F3); then one transaction deletes the `failed` rows and the retired rows beyond the newest `--keep`. The pointer's row is never selected whatever its status (`live_marked_retired` reports it; `rb index` warns on stderr); a row still naming a graph is left for the next run; `building` rows are left to `rb gc`. `delete_repo` drops one graph per transaction (phase 0: 80 graphs in one transaction exhaust the lock table).
- **`rb index --keep N`** (0-20, `RB_KEEP_RETIRED`, default 2), bounded at parse time.
- **Tests** (`rb-store/src/db_tests.rs`, database): `retention_holds_over_random_sequences` (seeded, 48 steps of publish / injected failure after the COPY / `--keep` change over two repositories; after each step the pointer, live, retired, failed and building rows and the `rb_<repo>_*` graphs equal a model), `a_publisher_killed_before_commit_leaves_no_row` (`pg_terminate_backend` from a test-only hook after the COPY), `a_failed_publish_records_a_failed_row`, `retention_never_selects_the_live_snapshot` (graph loop and row delete each with their own row); `keep_is_validated_at_parse_time` (CLI). The test cleanup also drops graphs a failed test left without a row.
- **Mutations** (`var/m5/mut/p2*.json`): 9/9 killed, including the two pre-M5 behaviours (a `building` row committed before the transaction: the killed-publisher and property tests fail; retired snapshots keeping their graphs: the property and pointer tests fail); a first off-by-one mutant that broke the SQL was replaced by a typed one.
- **Invariant 1:** fixture dumps byte-identical to `afae548`; no golden changed (none prints snapshot counts).
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 567 unit tests outside e2e (7 database tests vacuous without the URL), `rb-store` with the database 48/48; e2e 18/18 (`--nocapture`, no `skipping`); stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: `crates/rb-store/src/{lib,db_tests}.rs`, `crates/rb-cli/src/main.rs`, `tasks/todo.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-07] (wall clock) M5 phase 1: whole-snapshot reads and the AGE DDL lock (S1, S2)
- **S1:** `Store::read_tx` runs a closure's statements in one `REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY` transaction (a nested call joins it; `begin()` refuses a write inside it; `cypher` refuses to nest); `Store::read_snapshot` adds `snapshot {id} does not exist` when the snapshot was gone before the transaction started. `load_graph`, `load_diagnostics` and `status` read through it; the CLI's `status`, `dump`, `find`, `service`, `table-deps`, `callers`, `impact` and `path` read the live pointer and the snapshot in one transaction (`on_live`).
- **S2:** `RB_AGE_DDL_LOCK` (`pg_advisory_xact_lock`) before `create_graph` and before every `drop_graph`; `delete_repo` and retention take the repository row first, the publishers' order.
- **Tests** (new `crates/rb-store/src/db_tests.rs`, database via `RB_TEST_DATABASE_URL`, skipped without it): `a_snapshot_deleted_during_a_read_is_read_whole` (a test-only hook deletes the repository between the node and edge statements; failed first: nodes and no edges), `a_read_transaction_is_one_snapshot_and_read_only` (a concurrent flip is invisible; a write inside is refused; failed first), `graph_ddl_waits_for_the_ddl_lock` (a publish and a delete wait while another session holds the lock; failed first).

- **RTA (Y1, Y2, Y7, Y12, Y14):** string and text words also yield each `$` segment (`Outer$Inner` mentions `Inner`); S9 (d) also reads the word scan of every non-binary file (Python, Go, `.txt`, lock files, `META-INF/services`); a supertype head is `supertype` only for a type that is a definition (local classes in lambdas/anonymous bodies and class expressions give `other`, a class expression's name is no declaration); new fact `FileFacts.module_objects` (`import * as`, `export *`, `require`, `import()`, `*` when computed) keeps every TS/JS type of a module it may name; a row kills mutant R4.

- **Packages (Y8–Y11):** a Maven artifact declared twice with different versions, or a property defined twice, gives one `heuristic` edge to `@?` with `versions`; npm aliases key the real package (`alias`); `go.mod` `replace` applies by Go's rule (same module: its version, `replaced`; a directory or another module: `@?`).

- **`rb_store::rollup::table_rollup`** (new module; S14, D12, D5; amendment 12): from the `QUERIES` sources of the table, a breadth-first caller walk (`CALLS`, `REFERENCES` and dispatch at `--precision`, default `cha`; depth 25, node cap 200,000, each cut named); labels (operations, the services holding the queries, the best path certainty) flow from callee to caller until nothing grows; per service: ops, `direct` or `indirect via`, endpoints of reached handlers (inline handlers only through a step inside their spans), other entry points by kind, best certainty. **Through APIs:** `rb path`'s upstream continuation (`path::upstream_of`, factored out of `request_path`) from every endpoint reaching the table, up to `--hops`; a service already listed is not repeated; per service the best certainty, then the shortest chain.

## [2026-10-06] (wall clock) M4b phase 7: entry kinds and orphan eligibility
- **`rb-indexer/src/liveness.rs`** (new; S11 stored kinds, S12, D10; amendment 10), run after every edge producer:
  - **Entry kinds** (`Symbol` attribute `entry`, sorted, only when non-empty), import-gated: `main` (Java `static void main` with one `String[]` or no parameter binding; Go `func main()` in package `main`), `test` (JUnit 4/5 annotations; Go `Test`/`Benchmark`/`Fuzz`/`Example` + non-lower-case in `_test.go`; Python `test_*` in test files), `scheduled`, `listener` (`@EventListener`, `@TransactionalEventListener`, `@KafkaListener`, `@RabbitListener`, `@JmsListener`), `lifecycle` (`@PostConstruct`, `@PreDestroy`, `@Bean`, `run` of a `CommandLineRunner`/`ApplicationRunner`).
  - **`orphan_eligible: true`** only when, in order: a deployable service (`library`), not a test path (`test`), no entry kind and no inbound `HANDLED_BY` (`entry`), no non-inert annotation on it or an enclosing type (`annotation`), no convention-invoked name (`convention`: the S12 list plus `constructor`, accessors, Python dunders, every Go method), not a TS/JS default export (`default_export`), every declared supertype of it or an enclosing type resolves to a repository type (`supertype`), and every whole-word occurrence of its name in every non-binary file is its own declaration or inside the evidence of an edge into it (`mention`).
  - **The word scan** (`Words`): `index_commit` reads every non-binary blob once more (uncached), word = ASCII letters/digits/`_`; a file over 32 MiB or a name seen over 256 times keeps symbols alive; names with other characters are never eligible. One `orphan_coverage` per snapshot (`symbols`, `eligible`, `kept` per rule).
  - `GraphBuilder::set_node_attr` and `GraphBuilder::inbound` (rb-model); `rb_indexer::PIPELINE` is `m4b-di-4`.
- **Tests** (new module; written with it, then bitten by the mutations): `liveness::tests::{entry_kinds_are_import_gated, one_keeping_row_per_rule, orphan_buckets_sum_to_the_symbols, eligibility_grows_linearly}` (counted, 100 vs 400), `graph::tests::node_attributes_and_inbound_evidence`.
- **Mutations** (`var/m4b/mut/p7.json`, `p7b.json`): 29 mutants, 24 killed first; survivors `evidence-path`, `main-array`, `go-test-path`, `py-test`, `runner-gate` each got a row (an occurrence in another file at the evidence's position, a `List<String>` main, `TestB` outside `_test.go`, `pyhelper` in a test file, an unimported `CommandLineRunner`); re-run with a `decl-path` mutant: **6/6 killed** (35 in all).

No edge line changed.

## [2026-10-06] (wall clock) M4b phase 6: precision at query time
- **`rb_store::code::Precision`** (`rta`, `cha`; S10, D5). `View::with_precision` holds the mode; `View::dispatches(e)` skips an `IMPLEMENTS` step whose edge carries `impl_instantiated: false` under `rta` and records it once per `(implementing, implemented)` pair. Every dispatch expansion goes through it: `View::overridden` (callers of overridden methods in `rb callers`, `rb impact`'s dispatch and its walk, `rb path` upstream) and `path::Walk::implementations` (`rb path` downstream). Requiring edges are untouched: an implementation of a removed interface method still breaks.
- **Answers carry it:** `CallerTree`, `Impact` and `RequestPath` gain `precision` and `pruned_by_rta`. Text output prints `precision=<p>` after the title (and after `rb impact`'s `with` lines) and, when steps were skipped, `rta: <k> dispatch step(s) pruned (implementations never instantiated; --precision cha shows them)` (singular for 1, as `1 SCIP job`). `rb path --format json` gains `precision` and `pruned_by_rta`. The count is of steps skipped, an upper bound on what `cha` adds (a skipped step whose callers are reached anyway is still counted; unit comment).
- **CLI:** `--precision rta|cha` on `rb callers` (default `rta`), `rb impact` (default `cha`), `rb path` (default `rta`). `rb table-deps` gets the flag in phase 9 with the caller walk it would govern; before that it expands no dispatch.
- **Tests first** (did not compile before): `code::tests::rta_prunes_flagged_dispatch_steps_and_counts_them` (one graph under both modes: the flagged step skipped and counted under `rta`, taken under `cha`; a kept step taken under both; `rb impact` affected set under both; breaks unchanged), `path::tests::rta_prunes_dispatch_both_ways` (downstream and upstream, default `rta`), `rb` `code::tests::precision_header_counts_pruned_steps`.
- **Mutations** (`var/m4b/mut/p6.json`, same harness): pruning off, pruned step not recorded, downstream filter removed, `overridden` filter removed, `callers` ignoring the mode, any non-`true` flag treated as proven: **6/6 killed**.
- **Goldens re-blessed** (classified with `git diff -U0`: the only added lines are the header): `precision=rta` in 11 (`callers-*`, `scip-callers-*`, `path-login.txt`, `path-mfa-email.txt`, `scip-path-login.txt`), `precision=cha` in 9 (`impact-*`, `scip-impact-*`), `"precision": "rta"` and `"pruned_by_rta": 0` in `path-login.json`. No fixture type is proven yet (phase 5a), so no step is pruned and no tree changed. SCIP goldens re-blessed by the real container run (no `skipping:`).

## [2026-10-06] (wall clock) M4b phase 5a: RTA proof
- **`rb-indexer/src/rta.rs`** (new; S9, D4): every Java or TS/JS type that is (or contains) the source of an `IMPLEMENTS` edge is proven never instantiated only when, in order:
  - it is in a deployable service and not in a test source;
  - the repository has no unnamed-instantiation surface (a `beans.xml`, `includeFilters`, an `org.reflections`/`io.github.classgraph` import, `ClassPathScanningCandidateComponentProvider`);
  - every mention of its name is a declaration, an import or a supertype head, and no static import names it;
  - no string literal and no unparsed non-inert file holds the name (or a file was over the cap);
  - neither it nor a member carries an annotation or decorator outside the inert list;
  - it is not default-exported and does not implement a Spring Data fragment interface by name;
  - no type naming it as a supertype (by simple name, any file, tests included) is instantiated (breadth-first propagation over names with a visited set).
  - The proven types' `IMPLEMENTS` edges, and their methods', get `impl_instantiated: false` through `GraphBuilder::set_edge_attr` once every producer has run (`edge_sources` lists the sources). One `rta_coverage` per snapshot holds `types`, `proven`, `kept` (first failing rule, zeros included) and `prunable_edges`. `rb_indexer::PIPELINE` is `m4b-di-3`. Amendment 9.

- **`rb-indexer/src/di.rs`** (new; S4–S8 under F2): every injection point of the snapshot's Java files resolved to beans:
  - **Scope (D8):** main sources only (test points are bucket `test`, test beans are no candidates); classpath(S) from `via=package` edges (deployable services excluded); component roots from exactly one `@SpringBootApplication`/`@ComponentScan` class (else `scan_roots_unknown`), repository roots from `@Enable*Repositories` (else the component roots), Feign beans only under `@EnableFeignClients` roots of the same service.
  - **Candidates (S4):** Tier B type resolution of the injected type; beans indexed by every type they are assignable to (breadth-first supertype closure, depth 64, visited set); `@Bean` methods through their return type, else the types their body creates; external types through a same-named `@Bean` return type.
  - **Narrowing (D9):** `@Resource` name, qualifier (bean names and qualifiers), `@Primary`, name fallback; collections take every candidate; `Optional`-like wrappers make no candidate `optional_absent`.
  - **Gates (S5/S6):** `surface` (gate D over main sources and unparsed files), `mention` (gate M over every Java file's mentions and unparsed words), `generic`, `annotation_unread`, `bean_type_inferred`, `external_type`, `by_name`, `scan_roots_unknown`, `scan_unread`, `classpath_unknown`. They decide `plain` vs `capped` and `capped_by`; every edge is `heuristic` or `ambiguous` (F2).

## [2026-10-06] (wall clock) M4b phase 2: Spring facts, pure
- **`rb_frameworks::di`** (new; S2, S3, S6 gate D), a pure function of one Java file's facts plus M2b's repository bases:
  - **Beans:** stereotype classes (`@Component`, `@Service`, `@Repository`, `@Controller`, `@RestController`, `@ControllerAdvice`, `@RestControllerAdvice`, `@Configuration`, and `@SpringBootApplication`/`@SpringBootConfiguration`), concrete and independent only; `@Bean` methods of bean classes (return type, `created` types from `new` in the body); Spring Data repositories (M2b's bases plus store bases imported from `org.springframework.data.*`, not `@NoRepositoryBean`, no own type parameters; `extends` for fragments); `@FeignClient` interfaces. Names per D9 (`Introspector.decapitalize`), `@Qualifier` literals, `@Primary` (Feign's `primary` default), `@Profile`/`@Conditional*` (class or method), annotations outside the inert and DI-understood lists.
  - **Injection points:** the only constructor or the one marked among several (declared, Lombok-generated, or a record's canonical); `@Autowired`/`@Inject`/`@Resource` fields and methods (in any class: `not_a_bean` when the class is no bean); `@Bean` parameters. Wrappers (`List`/`Set`/`Collection`, `Map<String, T>`, arrays; `Optional`, `ObjectProvider`, `ObjectFactory`, `Provider`, all import-gated), `generic` for other type arguments, `@Qualifier`, `@Resource` names; `@Value`, `String`, boxed and primitive types excluded.
  - **Scans:** `@SpringBootApplication`/`@ComponentScan`, `@Enable*Repositories`, `@EnableFeignClients` roots (literals, class literals, else the class's package) and `unread` for any other attribute or a non-literal.
  - **Gate D:** gated-package imports (static, wildcard) outside the plain set, qualified annotations and supertypes, any other qualified `org.springframework` use; `surface_text` for files without facts (`spring.factories`/`*.imports`, `beans.xml`, the beans XML namespace, the raw word list as `@Word` or `Word(`, over the byte cap; prose and npm lock files inert).
  - `is_inert` (S12's list, package-gated except `NonNull`/`Nullable`) is shared for phases 5 and 7.
- **Facts (amendment 6):** `Binding.modifiers` and `Binding.initialised` (Java), for Lombok's rules; `clients::inert` made crate-visible.

## [2026-10-06] (wall clock) M4b phase 1b: default exports and mentions
- **New neutral facts** (`rb-syntax`; new module `mentions.rs`, fingerprinted; `imports.rs`):
  - `FileFacts.default_export` (TS/JS): the identifier of `export default x;`, or the name `export default class X`/`function f` declares; `None` for an expression, an anonymous class or `export { a as default }`.
  - `FileFacts.mentions` (Java, TS/JS): every identifier, type identifier and property-name token's text → its set of `MentionRole`s in the file (`declaration`, `import`, `supertype`, `type`, `new`, `class_literal`, `method_ref`, `member`, `annotation`, `logger`, `string`, `other`), plus every whole word of a string fragment. Roles come from the innermost structure a token sits in (a qualified name's segments share the position's role; a class literal that is the only argument of `getLogger(…)` is `logger`; a decorator's callee is `annotation`; `new m.T()` gives `T` `new` and `m` `member`). Comments are not mentions. Python and Go record none.
  - **Amendment 5:** the source name of an aliased specifier (`{ T as U }`) and `export default T;` are `other`, since the class is then instantiated under another name; JavaScript's `class_heritage` head is `supertype`.
- **Tests first:** `mentions::tests::{java_one_row_per_role, java_qualified_names_logger_and_two_roles, typescript_one_row_per_role}` failed against a stub returning nothing; `default_export_tests::default_exports_by_form` failed with the fact disabled (restored, bytes compared); `python_and_go_record_no_mentions` passes by construction. The JavaScript heritage row was added with its rule.
- **Verification:** fmt, clippy clean; 486 unit tests (rb-syntax 88; others unchanged); e2e 17/17 with the database and containers, no golden changed; stub scan: only `rb-sql/src/lib.rs:1111`.
- Files: `crates/rb-syntax/src/{lib,mentions,imports}.rs`, `tasks/{contract-m4b,todo}.md`, `IMPLEMENT.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## [2026-10-06] (wall clock) Fix M4a evaluator pass 5: every carrier gated
- Evaluator pass 5 (`tasks/eval-m4a-pass5.md`, now tracked; VERDICT FAIL against `2a20746`) fixed as the orchestrator decided: contract amendment 15 (Gate A covers every request: a `synthetic` stitch needs a provably plain carrier, whatever the URL's source), amendment 13's *Effect* line and amendment 14's request-call list and inline rule corrected, open item 22 rewritten with the M5 follow-up "measure the cost of amendments 14–15 on the M5 benchmark repositories", one new lesson. `rb_indexer::PIPELINE` is `m4a-http-9`.
  - **Facts** (`rb-syntax`; fingerprinted): `TokenRole::Member {name}` (TS/JS `x.m`, Go `x.M`, not called); name tokens also for TS/JS default and namespace imports of a package, the global objects `globalThis`/`window`/`global`/`self`, and Go imports; `Init::New {type_name, args}` for a Java object creation that roots no call chain. Tests `name_tokens_of_configured_names` (one row now `~defaults`, the `axios` import recorded), `module_and_global_object_tokens`.
  - **U1 (blocker)** (`clients.rs`): `mutate` is no longer a request call, and a request on a binding is plain only when the request chain's own links before the verb are `PLAIN_LINKS` (`x.mutate().apply/filter/requestInterceptor(…)…` → `heuristic`, `base: unverified`).
  - **U2 (major)** (`clients.rs` `verdict`, `SpringBases::carrier`, `ClientCall.module_call`, `global_hooks`; `stitch.rs`): the carrier is judged for every site, not only when a base was read: a Java field, parameter or injected instance, an instance with a non-plain construction link but no base, an exported/imported instance, a non-plain request config, a Go `http.Client` binding or `NewRequest` caps the site at `heuristic`, `base: unverified`, also with an absolute or configured URL. Bare module clients (`fetch`, axios's default export, Go's package and `http.DefaultClient`) stay plain unless some file of the repository reconfigures the module (`globalThis.fetch = …`, any non-request use of the axios module name such as `axios.defaults…`/`axios.interceptors…`, `http.DefaultTransport`). A method-local `new RestTemplate()` is a verifiable carrier (`Init::New`). **bk8** (a `RestTemplate` field, pass 3's `billing2` layout) becomes `heuristic`; a Feign client would stay `synthetic`.
  - **U3 (minor):** an axios `create` or request config is plain only when every key is in `PLAIN_AXIOS_KEYS` and it has no spread (`adapter`, `transformRequest`, `transformResponse`, `httpAgent`, `httpsAgent`, `proxy`, `lookup`, `socketPath`, … are not).
  - **M9:** `chain_base_reads_a_fresh_root` pins `fresh_root` in `chain_base`.
  - **Tests first:** `every_carrier_is_gated_whatever_the_url` (rb-indexer) was run against a `git archive` of `2a20746`: it failed with 13 of its 14 repro sites `synthetic` (only `p18` was `heuristic`). The new rb-frameworks and rb-syntax units did not compile there (new fields, roles and functions). Changed expectations, each by the rule: field and injected rows of `rest_template_web_client_and_rest_client` and `spring_client_bases_visible_on_the_instance` (base unknown, carrier a field), non-literal and spread request configs in `axios_request_configs_state_the_base`, `api5` in `instance_bases_are_verified_only_when_unchanged` (spread before `baseURL`), `NewRequest`/`NewRequestWithContext`/`c.Head` in `go_clients_and_sprintf`, and `port_holes_keep_the_host_in_every_source_form`'s Java client (now a method-local `new RestTemplate()`, so the row still tests the host).
  - **Mutations** (scratchpad harness: scripted edit, `rb-syntax`/`rb-frameworks`/`rb-indexer` `--lib`, restore, byte compare; every run printed its `test result` lines): 40 mutants, all killed — `mutate` allowed again; request-chain links not judged; `new RestTemplate(arg)` and a subclass plain; `Init::New` unreadable; every axios key plain; a non-literal config plain; inline and instance calls ignoring the request config; request configs never judged; an unread `request(cfg)` plain; axios module calls, `axios(…)` and `fetch` not flagged as module calls; every Go call bare; the fetch hook ignored; any receiver on axios plain; an axios member plain; a callee, a declaration and a `var` require counted as hooks; Go `DefaultTransport` ignored, `DefaultClient` always and never a hook; hooks never cap; every module call capped; an imported instance plain; no `Member` role; relative imports, the blank Go import and globals recorded wrongly; a Go selector call read as a member; `new` no token candidate; no `Init::New`; M9 (`fresh_root` → `true`); configuring chains not judged; a fresh inline chain always plain; a `RestTemplate` receiver always plain and an inline one never; a fluent receiver always plain. Two guards that each stop U1 (`mutate` dropped, chain links judged) masked each other, so each has its own row (`m1`, `l9`).
  - **Public repositories** (release binaries of `2a20746` and this change, `--no-scip`, `--force --no-cache`; `--force` cached `cmp`-identical on all seven; no host paths): piggymetrics and piomin byte-identical (Feign). No edge added or removed, no endpoint or certainty changed; no `synthetic` `CONSUMES` before or after. Changed: `base` `literal` → `unverified` on 7 edges — petclinic `VisitsServiceClient#getVisitsForPets` (injected `WebClient.Builder` field), `AIDataProvider` ×3 (`RestClient` field), `VectorStoreController` (`WebClient` field), ftgo `OrderServiceProxy#findOrderById` ×2 (`ambiguous`, injected `WebClient` field) — and `bases.unverified`: petclinic 0 → 6, node-express 0 → 1 (its e2e setup sets `axios.defaults.baseURL`, a real global hook; the site stays `self`), gin 0 → 51 (all `http.NewRequest` in tests; buckets unchanged), ftgo 0 → 1, Store 0 → 1 (the gateway filter's injected builder; `self`). Carriers by category: polyglot 7 bare + 1 Feign; petclinic 1 bare + 6 capped; node-express 1 bare, hooked; gin 51 capped; piggymetrics 4 Feign; ftgo 4 bare + 2 verified + 1 capped; piomin 4 Feign; Store 19 verified + 1 capped.

- Evaluator pass 4 (`tasks/eval-m4a-pass4.md`, now tracked; VERDICT FAIL against `b75b6fc`) fixed as the orchestrator decided: contract amendment 14 (Gate A narrowed to what a conservative scan can prove), amendment 13's Gate A claim corrected, open items 14 and 22 rewritten, the 2026-10-06 lesson on folded values rewritten. `rb_indexer::PIPELINE` is `m4a-http-8`.
  - **Facts** (`rb-syntax/src/tokens.rs`, replacing `uses.rs`; fingerprinted): `FileFacts.name_tokens`, one `NameToken {name, span, role}` per identifier token of a candidate name (a binding initialised by a call, or the simple target of an assignment from a call) anywhere in the file; role `declaration` (a non-exported TS/JS `const`/`let` declarator, a Java local variable declarator), `receiver {call}` (no optional chaining) or `other`. `FileFacts.uses`/`NameUse` deleted.   - **T1, T2, T3 (blockers), Gate A** (`rb-frameworks/src/clients.rs` `Gate`, `rb-indexer/src/stitch.rs`): a constructed base is verified only when every token of the binding's name in its whole scope (the enclosing function or method; for a top-level `const`, the file), before the declaration and in nested code too, is its one declaration or a request call's receiver. Fields, members, parameters, `var`s, module `let`s, exported instances (and so imported ones) and names declared twice are never verified. Deleted: `Uses` (resolved uses, receivers, unbound escapes), `FileClients.escaped_imports`, `ClientCall.top_instance`, `stitch::escaped_instances`, `SpringBases::builder_unchanged`.

Test `uri_builder_factories_override_the_base_and_unverify_it`.
  - **T5 (major)** (`fold::conditional_document`, formerly `profile_document`): any `spring.config.activate.*` key or the legacy `spring.profiles` (value or list) makes a document conditional; `spring.profiles.active` alone does not. `only_the_base_document_turns_the_config_client_off` gains `bk7`, `bk6` and a `spring.profiles.active` base document.
